attempt 2:
- We split the RanniGPTv1 env.yml file, making sure we had pip==24.0 at the top of the conda "dependencies" section
- We took the packages for pip to install out and put them into a new requirements.txt (used chatgpt canvas to carefully remove the indentations and hyphenations to look like a normal requirements.txt)
- Installed conda section with conda env create (doesn't have a --no-deps option like conda create and conda install, comparing line counts for env.yml vs readout afterwards was 89(?) vs 93 lines, so maybe installed 4 things maybe not idk man)
- Then installed pip section using pip install --no-deps -r "RanniGPTv1-pip-req.txt"
- For whatever reason it still said installing build dependencies for cutlet, fairseq, and tts
- Then we got 2 read timeout errors. Running it again fixed this issue
- Then we got errors saying it failed to build wheels for fairseq and tts. This is likely caused by the fact that as it mentioned we need the C++ build tools
- and anyway we want the editable (via git clone) version of TTS so we will do those 2 things first before attempting again, just good to see that we got past the error


So next time I believe we do things in this order:
- Install build tools and git clone tts repo
- Install conda-only part, with pip==24.0 (conda env create -f)
- ACTIVATE ENV !
- Install pip-only part, WITHOUT tts package in the requirements (we want editable version)
- Install editable version of tts package
